      <title>Literary Collective</title>
      <description><![CDATA[<p>Join a community of readers for quarterly gatherings to discuss everything books and reading. Our autumn get-together will feature a book tasting, refreshments and brief discussion about what we&rsquo;re all reading. No books will be assigned and challenges are optional. This no-pressure literary event is for adults who love reading. Registration is required; call the library to sign up.</p>
]]></description>
